(Linear combination).
Consider processes
\{Y_{t,i}\}
for
i\in[n]
that are weakly
\mathcal{M}
-dependent in
L^{\nu}
, each with rate function
\delta_{i}(m)
. Their linear combination, i.e.
\{\sum_{i=1}^{n}a_{i}Y_{t,i}\}
for some given constant coefficients
a_{i}
,
i\in[n]
, is also weakly
\mathcal{M}
-dependent in
L^{\nu}
, with the rate function

\delta(m)=\sum_{i=1}^{n}|a_{i}|\delta_{i}(m).

(ii)

(Product of linear processes).
Consider two (one-sided) linear processes
Y_{t}=\sum_{j\geq 0}a_{j}\epsilon_{t-j}
and
X_{t}=\sum_{i\geq 0}b_{i}\varepsilon_{t-j}
, with i.i.d. innovations
\{\epsilon_{t}\}_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
and
\{\varepsilon_{t}\}_{t\in\mathbb{Z}}
which are mutually independent at all lags. Assume that
\{Y_{t}\}
satisfies

(a)

\mathbb{E}(|\epsilon_{0}|^{2\nu})<\infty

for some
\nu\geq 1
;

(b)

There exists some constant
c_{\beta}>0
depending on
\beta\geq 0
such that

\sup_{m\geq 0}(m+1)^{\beta}\sum_{s=m}^{\infty}|a_{s}|\leq c_{\beta}.

Assume analogous conditions for
\{X_{t}\}
(with the same
\nu
and
\beta
without loss of generality). Then
\{Y_{t}\}
and
\{X_{t}\}
are weakly
\mathcal{M}
-dependent in
L^{2\nu}
, and
\{Y_{t}X_{t}\}
is weakly
\mathcal{M}
-dependent in
L^{\nu}
, all with the rate function
\delta(m)=m^{-\beta}
.

(iii)

(Squared linear process).
Consider
\{Y_{t}\}
defined in (ii) above. Let
\beta>0
. Then the process
\{Y_{t}^{2}\}
is also weakly
\mathcal{M}
-dependent in
L^{\nu}
with the rate function
\delta(m)=m^{-\beta}
.

Proof of Lemma
B.15
.

Part (i) is straightforward from Definition
B.2
.

For part (ii), the statement for the processes
\{Y_{t}\}
and
\{X_{t}\}
is direct from Section 3.3 in
13
. For the product process
\{Y_{t}X_{t}\}
, define
Z_{t}=Y_{t}X_{t}
and

Z_{t}^{(m)}=\sum_{j=0}^{\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}\sum_{i=0}^{\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}a_{j}b_{i}\epsilon_{t-j}\varepsilon_{t-i}.

Then by Minkowski’s inequality, Hölder’s inequality and the independence between
\epsilon_{t}
and
\varepsilon_{s}
for any
t,s
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|Z_{t}-Z_{t}^{(m)}\right\|_{\nu}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}a_{j}b_{i}\epsilon_{t-j}\varepsilon_{t-i}-\sum_{j=0}^{\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}\sum_{i=0}^{\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}a_{j}b_{i}\epsilon_{t-j}\varepsilon_{t-i}\right\|_{\nu}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left(\sum_{j\geq 0}a_{j}\epsilon_{t-j}\right)\left(\sum_{i>\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}b_{i}\varepsilon_{t-i}\right)\right\|_{\nu}+\left\|\left(\sum_{j>\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}a_{j}\epsilon_{t-j}\right)\left(\sum_{i\geq 0}b_{i}\varepsilon_{t-i}\right)\right\|_{\nu}

\displaystyle\;\quad+\left\|\left(\sum_{j>\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}a_{j}\epsilon_{t-j}\right)\left(\sum_{i>\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}b_{i}\varepsilon_{t-i}\right)\right\|_{\nu}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{j\geq 0}a_{j}\epsilon_{t-j}\right\|_{2\nu}\left\|\sum_{i>\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}b_{i}\varepsilon_{t-i}\right\|_{2\nu}+\left\|\sum_{j>\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}a_{j}\epsilon_{t-j}\right\|_{2\nu}\left\|\sum_{i\geq 0}b_{i}\varepsilon_{t-i}\right\|_{2\nu}

\displaystyle\;\quad+\left\|\sum_{j>\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}a_{j}\epsilon_{t-j}\right\|_{2\nu}\left\|\sum_{i>\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}b_{i}\varepsilon_{t-i}\right\|_{2\nu}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\sum_{j\geq 0}|a_{j}|\right)\left(\sum_{i>\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}|b_{i}|\right)+\left(\sum_{j>\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}|a_{j}|\right)\left(\sum_{i\geq 0}|b_{i}|\right)

\displaystyle\;\quad+\left(\sum_{j>\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}|a_{j}|\right)\left(\sum_{i>\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}|b_{i}|\right)\lesssim m^{-\beta},

where the last two lines used similar arguments in Section 3.3 of
13
and condition (b) which also implies
\sum_{j\geq 0}|a_{j}|+\sum_{i\geq 0}|b_{i}|\leq c
for some constant
c>0
. Noting that the definition of
Z_{t}^{(m)}
automatically fulfills (ii) of Definition
B.2
, the proof for part (ii) is complete.

Lastly, for part (iii), define
W_{t}=Y_{t}^{2}
, and

W_{t}^{(m)}=\sum_{j=0}^{\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}\sum_{i=0}^{\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}a_{j}a_{i}\epsilon_{t-j}\epsilon_{t-i}=\sum_{j=0}^{\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}a_{j}^{2}\epsilon_{t-j}^{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}\sum_{i=0;i\neq j}^{\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}a_{j}a_{i}\epsilon_{t-j}\epsilon_{t-i}.

Note that we can also write

W_{t}=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}a_{j}^{2}\epsilon_{t-j}^{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\sum_{i=0;i\neq j}^{\infty}a_{j}a_{i}\epsilon_{t-j}\epsilon_{t-i}.

Hence, similar to the proof for part (ii) above,

\displaystyle\left\|W_{t}-W_{t}^{(m)}\right\|_{\nu}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}a_{j}^{2}\epsilon_{t-j}^{2}-\sum_{j=0}^{\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}a_{j}^{2}\epsilon_{t-j}^{2}\right\|_{\nu}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\sum_{i=0;i\neq j}^{\infty}a_{j}a_{i}\epsilon_{t-j}\epsilon_{t-i}-\sum_{j=0}^{\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}\sum_{i=0;i\neq j}^{\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}a_{j}a_{i}\epsilon_{t-j}\epsilon_{t-i}\right\|_{\nu}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j>\left\lfloor m/2\right\rfloor}a_{j}^{2}+m^{-\beta}\lesssim m^{-2\beta}+m^{-\beta},

where the last line used the repetitive arguments as for part (ii) in the proof of this lemma, together with similar arguments in Section 3.3 of
13
. This concludes the proof of this lemma.
∎

Lemmas for controlling the partial sums.

Lemma B.16
.

Suppose that all the assumptions made in Lemma
B.10
hold.

(i)

For all
k\in[K]
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{k}}\bigl\lVert\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{k}\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}\bigr\rVert_{F}=\mathcal{O}_{P}\bigl(\alpha_{T,p,k}\bigl),\text{\ and\ }\frac{1}{p_{\text{-}k}}\bigl\lVert\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\bigr\rVert_{F}=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p,\text{-}k}).

(ii)

Let us write

\displaystyle H_{1}=\max_{k\in[K]}\|\widehat{H}_{k}\|_{,}\quad H_{2}=\max_{k\in[K]}\left\|\widehat{H}_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}^{\intercal}\right\|,\text{\ and \ }H_{3}=\max_{k\in[K]}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{k}}}\left\|\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\right\|_{F}.

With some finite constant
C_{H}>0
and
\alpha_{T,p}
in (
B.26
), let us define

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{T,p}=\left\{|H_{1}-1|\geq C_{H}\alpha_{T,p}\right\}\cup\left\{|H_{2}-1|\geq C_{H}\alpha_{T,p}\right\}\cup\left\{H_{3}\geq C_{H}\alpha_{T,p}\right\}.

Then we have
\mathbb{P}({\mathcal{H}}_{T,p})\to 0
as
\min(T,p_{1},\ldots,p_{K})\to\infty
.

Proof of Lemma
B.16
(i).

For any
k\in[K]
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{k}}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{k}\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}\right)=\frac{1}{p_{k}}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\right)\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}^{\intercal}+\frac{1}{p_{k}}\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\right)^{\intercal}+\frac{1}{p_{k}}\Lambda_{k}\left(\widehat{H}_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}^{\intercal}-I_{r_{k}}\right)\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}.

Lemma
B.10
shows that
\big\|\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\big\|_{F}=\mathcal{O}_{P}\bigl(\sqrt{p_{k}}\alpha_{T,p,k}\bigr)
, and since
\bigl\lVert\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}\bigr\rVert_{F}=\sqrt{r_{k}p_{k}}
and
\bigl\lVert\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}\bigr\rVert=\sqrt{p_{k}}
, we see

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{k}}\Big\|\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\right)\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}^{\intercal}\Big\|_{F}=\mathcal{O}_{P}\bigl(\alpha_{T,p,k}\bigr),\text{\ and \ }\frac{1}{p_{k}}\Big\|\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\bigl(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\bigr)^{\intercal}\Big\|_{F}=\mathcal{O}_{P}\bigl(\alpha_{T,p,k}\bigr).

Also, by Lemma
B.10
, we have

\widehat{H}_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}^{\intercal}=I_{r_{k}}+\mathcal{O}_{P}\bigl(\alpha_{T,p,k}\bigr)
, so that,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{k}}\Big\|\Lambda_{k}\left(\widehat{H}_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}^{\intercal}-I_{r_{k}}\right)\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}\Big\|_{F}\leq\frac{1}{p_{k}}\bigl\lVert\Lambda_{k}\bigr\rVert_{F}^{2}\bigl\lVert\widehat{H}_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}^{\intercal}-I_{r_{k}}\bigr\rVert_{F}=\mathcal{O}_{P}\bigl(\alpha_{T,p,k}\bigr).

Combining the above, we have

\frac{1}{p_{k}}\Big\|\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{k}\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}\Big\|_{F}=\mathcal{O}_{P}\bigl(\alpha_{T,p,k}\bigr).

(B.28)

For the second claim, it suffices to prove the result for
k=1
, for which

\widehat{\Lambda}_{-1}\widehat{\Lambda}_{-1}^{\intercal}=\otimes_{\ell=2}^{K}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\ell}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\ell}^{\intercal},\text{\ and \ }\Lambda_{-1}\Lambda_{-1}^{\intercal}=\otimes_{\ell=2}^{K}\Lambda_{\ell}\Lambda_{\ell}^{\intercal}.

By successively adding and subtracting intermediate terms, we obtain

\displaystyle\otimes_{\ell=2}^{K}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\ell}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\ell}^{\intercal}-\otimes_{\ell=2}^{K}\Lambda_{\ell}\Lambda_{\ell}^{\intercal}=\sum_{h=2}^{K}\Big(\otimes_{\ell=2}^{h-1}\Lambda_{\ell}\Lambda_{\ell}^{\intercal}\Big)\otimes\Big(\widehat{\Lambda}_{h}\widehat{\Lambda}_{h}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{h}\Lambda_{h}^{\intercal}\Big)\otimes\Big(\otimes_{\ell=h+1}^{K}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\ell}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\ell}^{\intercal}\Big).

Using
\|A\otimes B\|_{{F}}=\|A\|_{F}\|B\|_{F}
and the triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{-1}}\big\|\widehat{\Lambda}_{-1}\widehat{\Lambda}_{-1}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{-1}\Lambda_{-1}^{\intercal}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{-1}}\sum_{h=2}^{K}\Big(\prod_{\ell=2}^{h-1}\|\Lambda_{\ell}\Lambda_{\ell}^{\intercal}\|_{F}\Big)\,\big\|\widehat{\Lambda}_{h}\widehat{\Lambda}_{h}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{h}\Lambda_{h}^{\intercal}\big\|_{F}\,\Big(\prod_{\ell=h+1}^{K}\|\widehat{\Lambda}_{\ell}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\ell}^{\intercal}\|_{F}\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{P}\Big(\sum_{h=2}^{K}\alpha_{T,p,h}\Big)=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p,-1}),

from (
B.28
) and that
\|\Lambda_{\ell}\Lambda_{\ell}^{\intercal}\|_{F}=\mathcal{O}(p_{\ell})
under Assumption
2
(ii), and
\|\widehat{\Lambda}_{\ell}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\ell}^{\intercal}\|_{F}=\mathcal{O}(p_{\ell})
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
B.16
(ii).

By Lemma
B.10
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{H}_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}^{\intercal}\right\|

\displaystyle=1+\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p,k})=1+\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p}),\text{ \ and }

\displaystyle\|\widehat{H}_{k}\|

\displaystyle=1+\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p,k})=1+\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p}).

With
K
being finite, it follows that
|H_{1}-1|=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p})
,
|H_{2}-1|=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p})
, and
H_{3}=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p})
by Lemma
B.10
, so choosing
C_{H}>0
large enough gives
\mathbb{P}({\mathcal{H}}_{T,p})\to 0
as
\min(T,p_{1},\ldots,p_{K})\to\infty
.
∎

Lemma B.17
.

Suppose that all the assumptions made in Lemma
B.10
hold.
Then we have

(i)

p^{-1}\big\|\mathbb{E}\big(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\big\|_{F}=\mathcal{O}(1)
.

(ii)

{(pp_{\text{-}k})}^{-1}\big\|\mathbb{E}\big(E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\big\|_{F}=\mathcal{O}(1)
.

(iii)

(pp_{\text{-}k})^{-1}\big\|\mathbb{E}\big(X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\big\|_{F}=\mathcal{O}(1)
.

(iv)

For any
0\leq s<\tau<e\leq T
, we have

\frac{1}{p\,p_{\text{-}k}}\left(\frac{1}{e-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e}\mathbb{E}\big(E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)-\frac{1}{\tau-s}\sum_{t=s+1}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}\big(E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\right)=\mathbf{O}.

Proof of Lemma
B.17
(i).

Using
\mathbb{E}(G_{k,t})=\mathbb{E}(E_{k,t})=0
and the independence of
\{G_{k,t}\}
and
\{E_{k,t}\}
from Assumptions
1
and
4
,

\Big\|\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal})\Big\|_{F}\leq\Big\|\Lambda_{k}\,\mathbb{E}\big(G_{k,t}\,\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\,G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\,\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}\Big\|_{F}+\Big\|\mathbb{E}(E_{k,t}E_{k,t}^{\intercal})\Big\|_{F}=:U_{1}+U_{2}.

By Assumption
2
(ii), we have
\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}=\otimes_{\ell\neq k}(\Lambda_{\ell}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{\ell})=\otimes_{\ell\neq k}(p_{\ell}I_{r_{\ell}})=p_{\text{-}k}\,I_{r_{\text{-}k}}
. Therefore

\Lambda_{k}\,\mathbb{E}\big(G_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\,\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}=\Lambda_{k}\big[p_{\text{-}k}\,\mathbb{E}(G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big]\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}.

By sub-multiplicativity of the Frobenius norm and Jensen’s inequality, and recalling that the mode-
k
unfolding of
\mathcal{G}_{t}
is
G_{k,t}=\sum_{j=1}^{q+1}A_{j,k}F_{k,t}A_{j,\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\cdot\mathbb{I}_{\{\theta_{j-1}<t\leq\theta_{j}\}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\Big\|\Lambda_{k}\big[p_{\text{-}k}\,\mathbb{E}(G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big]\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}\Big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbb{E}(G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big\|_{F}\leq\mathbb{E}\big(\|G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\|_{F}\big)\leq\mathbb{E}\big(\|G_{k,t}\|_{F}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{q+1}\|A_{j-1,k}\|_{F}^{2}\mathbb{E}(\|F_{k,t}\|_{F}^{2})\|A_{j-1,-k}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\mathbb{I}_{\{\theta_{j-1}<t\leq\theta_{j}\}}.

Then by Assumptions
1
and
’
‣
A.3.1
(i), the LHS is of order
\mathcal{O}(r)
, and hence
U_{1}=\mathcal{O}(p)
.
As for
U_{2}
, by Assumption
4
, each entry of
E_{k,t}
has uniformly bounded variance and hence by Jensen’s inequality,

\displaystyle U_{2}=\big\|\mathbb{E}(E_{k,t}E_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big\|_{F}\leq\mathbb{E}\big(\|E_{k,t}\|_{F}^{2}\big)=\sum_{i=1}^{p_{k}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{p_{\text{-}k}}\mathbb{E}\big((E_{k,t})_{i\ell}^{2}\big)=\mathcal{O}(p).

(B.29)

∎

Proof of Lemma
B.17
(ii).

By Assumption
2
(ii), by Jensen’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\big\|\mathbb{E}(E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\mathbb{E}\Big(\big\|E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big\|_{F}\Big)\leq\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\big\|\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\big\|\mathbb{E}(\|E_{k,t}\|_{F}^{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p}\mathbb{E}(\|E_{k,t}\|_{F}^{2})=\mathcal{O}(1),

where the last equality follows from (
B.29
).
∎

Proof of Lemma
B.17
(iii).

Since
\mathbb{E}(G_{k,t})=\mathbb{E}(E_{k,t})=0
, and from the independence of
\{G_{k,t}\}
and
\{E_{k,t}\}
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\;\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\mathbb{E}\big(X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{k}}\Lambda_{k}\mathbb{E}\big(G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}+\frac{1}{p}\Lambda_{k}\mathbb{E}\big(G_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)+\frac{1}{p}\mathbb{E}\big(E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}+\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\mathbb{E}\big(E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{k}}\Lambda_{k}\mathbb{E}\big(G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}+\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\mathbb{E}\big(E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big).

Recalling the arguments in the proof of Lemma
B.17
(i), using Assumption
2
and Lemma
B.17
(ii), we have

\displaystyle\quad\;\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\big(X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{k}}\left\|\Lambda_{k}\mathbb{E}\big(G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}\right\|_{F}+\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\big(E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{k}}\|\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{k}\|\left\|\mathbb{E}\big(G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\right\|_{F}+\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\big(E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\right\|_{F}=\mathcal{O}(1),

which completes the proof.
∎

Proof of Lemma
B.17
(iv).

For any
i,j\in[p_{k}]
, we have

\displaystyle\left[\mathbb{E}\big(E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\right]_{ij}

\displaystyle=\sum_{u=1}^{p_{k}}\sum_{v=1}^{p_{k}}\left(\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)_{uv}\mathbb{E}\big((E_{k,t})_{iu}(E_{k,t}^{\intercal})_{jv}\big).

Under Assumption
4
, by the stationarity of
\{E_{k,t}\}
, we have
\mathbb{E}\big((E_{k,t})_{iu}(E_{k,t}^{\intercal})_{jv}\big)
is constant in
t
, for all
i,j,u,v
. Therefore, for any
\tau\in(s,e)
, we have

\frac{1}{p\,p_{\text{-}k}}\left(\frac{1}{e-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e}\mathbb{E}\big(E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)-\frac{1}{\tau-s}\sum_{t=s+1}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}\big(E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\right)=\mathbf{O}.

∎

Let us define

\displaystyle\widehat{V}_{t}=\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{V}^{(1)}_{t}\\
\vdots\\
\widehat{V}^{(K)}_{t}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{vech}\left(\widehat{G}_{1,t}\widehat{G}_{1,t}^{\intercal}\right)\\
\vdots\\
\mathrm{vech}\left(\widehat{G}_{K,t}\widehat{G}_{K,t}^{\intercal}\right)\end{bmatrix}\text{\ and\ }V_{t}=\begin{bmatrix}V^{(1)}_{t}\\
\vdots\\
V^{(K)}_{t}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{vech}\left(\widehat{H}_{1}^{\intercal}\mathbb{E}({G}_{1,t}{G}_{1,t}^{\intercal})\widehat{H}_{1}\right)\\
\vdots\\
\mathrm{vech}\left(\widehat{H}_{K}^{\intercal}\mathbb{E}({G}_{K,t}{G}_{K,t}^{\intercal})\widehat{H}_{K}\right)\end{bmatrix}.

(B.30)

For any
0\leq s<\tau<e\leq T
, define

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{V}}^{(k)}_{s,\tau,e}=\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s)(e-\tau)}{e-s}}\,\mathrm{vech}\left(\widehat{\Gamma}_{G,\tau,e}^{(k)}-\widehat{\Gamma}_{G,s,\tau}^{(k)}\right),

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}^{(k)}_{s,\tau,e}=\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s)(e-\tau)}{e-s}}\,\mathrm{vech}\left\{\widehat{H}_{k}^{\intercal}\left({\Gamma}_{G,\tau,e}^{(k)}-{\Gamma}_{G,s,\tau}^{(k)}\right)\widehat{H}_{k}\right\},

(B.31)

and

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{V}}_{s,\tau,e}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\mathcal{V}}^{(1)}_{s,\tau,e}\\
\vdots\\
\widehat{\mathcal{V}}^{(K)}_{s,\tau,e}\end{bmatrix}=\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s)(e-\tau)}{e-s}}\left(\frac{1}{e-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e}\widehat{V}_{t}-\frac{1}{\tau-s}\sum_{t=s+1}^{\tau}\widehat{V}_{t}\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}_{s,\tau,e}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}{\mathcal{V}}^{(1)}_{s,\tau,e}\\
\vdots\\
{\mathcal{V}}^{(K)}_{s,\tau,e}\end{bmatrix}=\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s)(e-\tau)}{e-s}}\left(\frac{1}{e-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e}V_{t}-\frac{1}{\tau-s}\sum_{t=s+1}^{\tau}V_{t}\right).

Lemma B.18
.

Let Assumptions
2
and
4
hold,
and recall that
\Theta
denotes the set of true change points.
Then we have the following decomposition

\displaystyle\quad\;\big|\widehat{\mathcal{V}}^{(k)}_{s,\tau,e}-{\mathcal{V}}^{(k)}_{s,\tau,e}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s)(e-\tau)}{e-s}}\left(\Big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e}^{(1)}-{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s,\tau}^{(1)}\Big\|_{F}+\Big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e}^{(2)}-{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s,\tau}^{(2)}\Big\|_{F}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,2\sqrt{2}\|\widehat{H}_{k}\|\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{k}}}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\right)\right\|_{F}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s)(e-\tau)}{e-s}}\cdot\left|\mathrm{vech}\left(\Gamma_{G,\tau,e}^{(k)}-\Gamma_{G,s,\tau}^{(k)}\right)\right|_{2},

where for any
0\leq a\leq b\leq T
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{J}}_{k,a,b}^{(1)}=\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{b-a}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}\left[X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}\left(X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)\right],

\displaystyle{\mathcal{J}}_{k,a,b}^{(2)}=\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{b-a}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}X_{k,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)X_{k,t}^{\intercal}.

Proof of Lemma
B.18
.

Let us denote by
C_{k,t}
the mode-
k
unfolding of
\mathcal{C}_{t}
. For any
0\leq a<b\leq T
, we have

\displaystyle{\Gamma}_{G,a,b}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{b-a}\frac{1}{p^{2}}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}\mathbb{E}\left({\Lambda}_{k}^{\intercal}C_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}C_{k,t}^{\intercal}{\Lambda}_{k}\right),

by Assumption
2
, and

\displaystyle\widehat{\Gamma}_{G,a,b}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{b-a}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}\widehat{G}_{k,t}\widehat{G}_{k,t}^{\intercal}=\frac{1}{p^{2}}\frac{1}{b-a}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p^{2}}\frac{1}{b-a}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}\Big\{\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}^{\intercal}\left(X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}\left(X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)\right)\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}\Big\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{p^{2}}\frac{1}{b-a}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}\Big\{\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}^{\intercal}\left[X_{k,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right]\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}\Big\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{p^{2}}\frac{1}{b-a}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}\Big\{\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}^{\intercal}\Big\{\mathbb{E}\left(X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(C_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}C_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)\Big\}\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}\Big\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{p^{2}}\frac{1}{b-a}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}\Big\{\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}^{\intercal}\mathbb{E}\left(C_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}C_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-(\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k})^{\intercal}\mathbb{E}\left(C_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}C_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)(\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k})\Big\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{p^{2}}\frac{1}{b-a}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}\Big\{(\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k})^{\intercal}\mathbb{E}\left(C_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}C_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)(\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k})\Big\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{k}}\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}^{\intercal}{\mathcal{J}}_{k,a,b}^{(1)}\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}+\frac{1}{p_{k}}\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}^{\intercal}{\mathcal{J}}_{k,a,b}^{(2)}\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}+\frac{1}{p_{k}}\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}^{\intercal}{\mathcal{J}}_{k,a,b}^{(3)}\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}+{\mathcal{J}}_{k,a,b}^{(4)}+\widehat{H}_{k}^{\intercal}\Gamma_{G,a,b}^{(k)}\widehat{H}_{k},

where

\displaystyle{\mathcal{J}}_{k,a,b}^{(3)}=\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{b-a}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}\Big\{\mathbb{E}\left(X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(C_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}C_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)\Big\},\text{\ and }

\displaystyle{\mathcal{J}}_{k,a,b}^{(4)}=\frac{1}{p^{2}}\frac{1}{b-a}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}\Big\{\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}^{\intercal}\mathbb{E}\left(C_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}C_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-(\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k})^{\intercal}\mathbb{E}\left(C_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}C_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)(\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k})\Big\}.

Then, for any
0\leq s<\tau<e\leq T
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\;\left|\widehat{\mathcal{V}}_{s,\tau,e}^{(k)}-{\mathcal{V}}_{s,\tau,e}^{(k)}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s)(e-\tau)}{e-s}}\left|\frac{1}{e-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e}\left(\widehat{V}_{t}^{(k)}-V_{t}^{(k)}\right)-\frac{1}{\tau-s}\sum_{t=s+1}^{\tau}\left(\widehat{V}_{t}^{(k)}-V_{t}^{(k)}\right)\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{1}{e-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e}\left[\widehat{G}_{k,t}\widehat{G}_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\widehat{H}_{k}^{\intercal}{\Gamma}_{G,\tau,e}^{(k)}\widehat{H}_{k}\right]-\frac{1}{\tau-s}\sum_{t=s+1}^{\tau}\left[\widehat{G}_{k,t}\widehat{G}_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\widehat{H}_{k}^{\intercal}{\Gamma}_{G,s,\tau}^{(k)}\widehat{H}_{k}\right]\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\left(\widehat{\Gamma}_{G,\tau,e}^{(k)}-\widehat{H}_{k}^{\intercal}\,{\Gamma}_{G,\tau,e}^{(k)}\,\widehat{H}_{k}\right)-\left(\widehat{\Gamma}_{G,s,\tau}^{(k)}-\widehat{H}_{k}^{\intercal}\,{\Gamma}_{G,s,\tau}^{(k)}\,\widehat{H}_{k}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e}^{(1)}-{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s,\tau}^{(1)}\right\|_{F}+\left\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e}^{(2)}-{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s,\tau}^{(2)}\right\|_{F}+\left\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e}^{(3)}-{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s,\tau}^{(3)}\right\|_{F}+\left\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e}^{(4)}-{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s,\tau}^{(4)}\right\|_{F}.

Since
\{E_{k,t}\}
and
\{C_{k,t}\}
are independent (Assumption
4
),
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(C_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}C_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)=\mathbb{E}\left(E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right).

Then for
\big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e}^{(3)}-{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s,\tau}^{(3)}\big\|_{F}
, by Lemma
B.17
(iv),

\displaystyle\quad\;\big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e}^{(3)}-{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s,\tau}^{(3)}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\Bigg\|\frac{1}{p^{2}}\frac{1}{e-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e}\Big\{\mathbb{E}\left(X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(C_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}C_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)\Big\}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad-\frac{1}{p^{2}}\frac{1}{\tau-s}\sum_{t=s+1}^{\tau}\Big\{\mathbb{E}\left(X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left(C_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}C_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)\Big\}\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{e-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e}\mathbb{E}\left(E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)-\frac{1}{\tau-s}\sum_{t=s+1}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}\left(E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)\Bigg\|_{F}=0.

By Assumption
2
,

\displaystyle\quad\;\big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e}^{(4)}-{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s,\tau}^{(4)}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg\|\frac{1}{p_{k}^{2}}\frac{1}{e-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e}\big(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\big)^{\intercal}\Lambda_{k}\mathbb{E}\big(G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}

\displaystyle\quad\quad-\frac{1}{p_{k}^{2}}\frac{1}{\tau-s}\sum_{t=s+1}^{\tau}\big(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\big)^{\intercal}\Lambda_{k}\mathbb{E}\big(G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\Bigg\|\frac{1}{p_{k}^{2}}\frac{1}{e-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e}\big(\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\big)^{\intercal}\Lambda_{k}\mathbb{E}\big(G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}\big(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\big)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-\frac{1}{p_{k}^{2}}\frac{1}{\tau-s}\sum_{t=s+1}^{\tau}\big(\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\big)^{\intercal}\Lambda_{k}\mathbb{E}\big(G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}\big(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\big)\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\widehat{H}_{k}\|\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{k}}}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\right)\right\|_{F}\cdot\Bigg\|\frac{1}{e-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e}\mathbb{E}\big(G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)-\frac{1}{\tau-s}\sum_{t=s+1}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}\big(G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle=2\|\widehat{H}_{k}\|\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{k}}}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\right)\right\|_{F}\cdot\Big\|\Gamma_{G,\tau,e}^{(k)}-\Gamma_{G,s,\tau}^{(k)}\Big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{2}\|\widehat{H}_{k}\|\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{k}}}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\right)\right\|_{F}\cdot\left|\mathrm{vech}\left(\Gamma_{G,\tau,e}^{(k)}-\Gamma_{G,s,\tau}^{(k)}\right)\right|_{2}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\quad\;\big|\widehat{\mathcal{V}}^{(k)}_{s,\tau,e}-{\mathcal{V}}^{(k)}_{s,\tau,e}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s)(e-\tau)}{e-s}}\left|\frac{1}{e-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e}\left(\widehat{V}_{t}^{(k)}-V_{t}^{(k)}\right)-\frac{1}{\tau-s}\sum_{t=s+1}^{\tau}\left(\widehat{V}_{t}^{(k)}-V_{t}^{(k)}\right)\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s)(e-\tau)}{e-s}}\left(\Big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e}^{(1)}-{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s,\tau}^{(1)}\Big\|_{F}+\Big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e}^{(2)}-{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s,\tau}^{(2)}\Big\|_{F}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,2\sqrt{2}\|\widehat{H}_{k}\|\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{k}}}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\right)\right\|_{F}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s)(e-\tau)}{e-s}}\cdot\left|\mathrm{vech}\left(\Gamma_{G,\tau,e}^{(k)}-\Gamma_{G,s,\tau}^{(k)}\right)\right|_{2}.

∎

Lemma B.19
.

Let Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
and
4
hold, with
\nu>8
and
\beta\geq 0
.
For
\mathbb{M}
given in (
3.4
),
let us re-index its elements as
\mathbb{M}=\{(s_{\ell},e_{\ell}],\,\ell\in|\mathbb{M}|\}
, and define

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}:=\left\{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}):\ell\in|\mathbb{M}|,\,\min(\tau-s_{\ell},e_{\ell}-\tau)>\varpi_{T}\right\}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\left\|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\big[(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big]\right\|_{F},\right.

\displaystyle\left.\frac{1}{\sqrt{e_{\ell}-\tau}}\left\|\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e_{\ell}}\big[(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big]\right\|_{F}\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{P}\left(\sqrt{\log(T)}\right).

Proof of Lemma
B.19
.

Recalling that the collection
\mathbb{M}
contains intervals grouped into levels
h=1,\ldots,\lfloor\mu_{T}\rfloor
, we write
\mathbb{M}=\cup_{h=1}^{\lfloor\mu_{T}\rfloor}\mathbb{M}_{h}
where
|\mathbb{M}_{h}|=\lfloor T/m_{h}\rfloor-1\asymp 2^{h}
and each interval therein has length
2m_{h}
, with
m_{h}=T/2^{h}
.
Each interval contributes
\mathcal{O}(m_{h})
admissible split points, so the total number of admissible triples
(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})
across all intervals is bounded by
\sum_{h=1}^{\lfloor\mu_{T}\rfloor}\mathcal{O}(2^{h}m_{h})=\mathcal{O}(T\log\log(T))
.

For
i,j\in[p_{k}]
, we have

\displaystyle[X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal})]_{ij}=p_{\text{-}k}^{-2}\sum_{u=1}^{p_{\text{-}k}}[X_{k,t,iu}X_{k,t,ju}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t,iu}X_{k,t,ju})].

Define
S_{k,t}(i,j;u,v)=X_{k,t,iu}X_{k,t,jv}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t,iu}X_{k,t,jv})
.
By Proposition 6.5 of
60
(with
B=0
and
T=\tau-s_{\ell}
therein) yields, for any
z>0
,
i,j\in[p_{k}]
and
u\in[p_{\text{-}k}]
,

\displaystyle\quad\;\mathbb{P}\Bigl(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\Big|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}S_{k,t}(i,j;u,v)\Big|\geq z\Bigr)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\nu,\beta}[\Phi_{\nu,\beta}(\mathcal{X})]^{\nu}(\tau-s_{\ell})^{-\nu/4+1}z^{-\nu/2}+C\exp\left(-\frac{z^{2}}{C_{\beta}[\Phi_{4,\beta}(\mathcal{X})]^{4}}\right),

where
C_{\nu,\beta},C
and
C_{\beta}
are constants depending only on
\nu
and
\beta
, and the uniform d.a.n.
\Phi_{\nu,\beta}(\mathcal{X})
is finite by Lemma
B.14
.

Let
L=\min_{\ell\in[|\mathbb{M}|]:s_{\ell}<\tau<e_{\ell}}\{\tau-s_{\ell},e_{\ell}-\tau\}
. By construction,
L\geq\varpi_{T}\asymp T/\log(T)
. Since the number of admissible triples is
\mathcal{O}(T\log(T))
, by Bonferroni correction, we have

\displaystyle\quad\;\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\Big|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}S_{k,t}(i,j;u,v)\Big|\geq z\right)

\displaystyle\leq T\log(T)\left\{C_{\nu,\beta}[\Phi_{\nu,\beta}(\mathcal{X})]^{\nu}(\tau-s_{\ell})^{-\nu/4+1}z^{-\nu/2}+C\exp\left(-\frac{z^{2}}{C_{\beta}[\Phi_{4,\beta}(\mathcal{X})]^{4}}\right)\right\}.

Then for any
\delta>0
,

\displaystyle\quad\;\mathbb{E}\left(\left\{\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\Big|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}S_{k,t}(i,j;u,v)\Big|\right\}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\Big|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}S_{k,t}(i,j;u,v)\Big|\geq\sqrt{z}\right)\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\leq\delta^{2}+\int_{\delta^{2}}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\Big|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}S_{k,t}(i,j;u,v)\Big|\geq\sqrt{z}\right)\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\leq\delta^{2}+T\log(T)\cdot C_{\nu,\beta}[\Phi_{\nu,\beta}(\mathcal{X})]^{\nu}L^{-\nu/4+1}\int_{\delta^{2}}^{\infty}z^{-\nu/4}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\qquad+T\log(T)\cdot C\int_{\delta^{2}}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{z}{C_{\beta}[\Phi_{4,\beta}(\mathcal{X})]^{4}}\right)\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{2}+\frac{4}{\nu-4}T\log(T)L^{-\nu/4+1}\delta^{2-\nu/2}+T\log(T)\exp\left(-\frac{\delta^{2}}{C_{\beta}[\Phi_{4,\beta}(\mathcal{X})]^{4}}\right).

Setting
\delta^{2}\geq 2C_{\beta}[\Phi_{4,\beta}(\mathcal{X})]^{4}\log(T)
, we have

T\log(T)\cdot L^{-\nu/4+1}\delta^{2-\nu/2}\asymp T^{-\nu/4+2}\log(T).

Hence, with
\nu>8
, the expectation is bounded by

\displaystyle\quad\;\mathbb{E}\left(\left\{\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\Big|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}S_{k,t}(i,j;u,v)\Big|\right\}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{2}+T^{2-\nu/4}\log(T)+T\log(T)\cdot\exp\left(-\log(T)\right)\lesssim\log(T),

where the hidden constant does not depend on
i
,
j
,
u
or
v
.
Thus we can derive by Cauchy–Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\quad\;\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\bigg|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\left[X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal})\right]_{ij}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\Bigg|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\sum_{u=1}^{p_{\text{-}k}}S_{k,t}(i,j;u,u)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\,\sum_{u=1}^{p_{\text{-}k}}\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\Bigg|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}S_{k,t}(i,j;u,u)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\,\sqrt{p_{\text{-}k}}\left(\sum_{u=1}^{p_{\text{-}k}}\left\{\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\Bigg|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}S_{k,t}(i,j;u,u)\Bigg|\right\}^{2}\right)^{1/2},

from which we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\left\{\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\left[X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal})\right]_{ij}\right\}^{2}\right)\lesssim p_{\text{-}k}^{2}\log(T),

where the hidden constant does not depend on
i
or
j
.
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left(\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\big[(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big]\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)\lesssim\log(T).

By Markov’s inequality, for any constant
C>0
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\;\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{p}\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\big[(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big]\right\|_{F}\geq C\sqrt{\log(T)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq{\frac{1}{p^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left(\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\big[(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big]\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)}(C^{2}\log(T))^{-1}\lesssim\frac{1}{C^{2}},

which shows that

{\frac{1}{p}\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\big[(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big]\right\|_{F}}=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\sqrt{\log(T)}).

Analogous arguments apply to the sums over
\{\tau+1,\ldots,e_{\ell}\}
uniformly over
\mathcal{I}
so that the conclusion follows.
∎

Lemma B.20
.

Let all assumptions in Lemma
B.19
hold. Define
Z_{k,t}=p_{-k}^{-1}X_{k,t}\Lambda_{-k}
and for
\mathcal{I}
defined in Lemma
B.19
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{T,p}^{(1)}=\left\{\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\max\left(\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}\Big|{\mathcal{J}}_{s_{\ell},\tau}^{(1)}\Big|_{2},\,\sqrt{e_{\ell}-\tau}\Big|{\mathcal{J}}_{\tau,e_{\ell}}^{(1)}\Big|_{2}\right)\geq C_{1}\sqrt{\log(T)}\right\},

with some finite constant
C_{1}>0
, where for any
0\leq a<b\leq T
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{J}}_{a,b}^{(1)}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{vech}\left({\mathcal{J}}_{1,a,b}^{(1)}\right)\\
\vdots\\
\mathrm{vech}\left({\mathcal{J}}_{K,a,b}^{(1)}\right)\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{vech}\left(\frac{1}{pp_{-1}}\frac{1}{b-a}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}Z_{1,t}Z_{1,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{1,t}Z_{1,t}^{\intercal})\right)\\
\vdots\\
\mathrm{vech}\left(\frac{1}{pp_{-K}}\frac{1}{b-a}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}Z_{K,t}Z_{K,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{K,t}Z_{K,t}^{\intercal})\right)\end{bmatrix}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{S}_{T,p}^{(1)}\right)\to 0\text{\ as\ }\min(T,p_{1},\ldots,p_{K})\to\infty.

Proof of Lemma
B.20
.

Note that with
\Lambda_{\text{-}k}=[\lambda_{-k,uv},\,u\in[p_{\text{-}k}],v\in[r_{\text{-}k}]]
, we have

\displaystyle[Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal})]_{i,j}=p_{-k}^{-2}\sum_{u,v=1}^{p_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\lambda_{-k,u\ell}\lambda_{-k,v\ell}\,[X_{k,t,iu}X_{k,t,jv}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t,iu}X_{k,t,jv})].

Recall
S_{k,t}(i,j;u,v)=X_{k,t,iu}X_{k,t,jv}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t,iu}X_{k,t,jv})
defined in the proof of Lemma
B.19
.
For any
i,j\in[p_{k}]
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\;\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\bigg|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\left[Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal})\right]_{i,j}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\Bigg|p_{\text{-}k}^{-2}\sum_{h=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{u=1}^{p_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{v=1}^{p_{\text{-}k}}\lambda_{-k,uh}\lambda_{-k,vh}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}S_{k,t}(i,j;u,v)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq p_{\text{-}k}^{-2}\sum_{h=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{u=1}^{p_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{v=1}^{p_{\text{-}k}}\big|\lambda_{-k,uh}\lambda_{-k,vh}\big|\cdot\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\Bigg|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}S_{k,t}(i,j;u,v)\Bigg|.

By Assumption
2
(i), we have

p_{\text{-}k}^{-2}\sum_{h=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{u=1}^{p_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{v=1}^{p_{\text{-}k}}\big|\lambda_{-k,uh}\lambda_{-k,vh}\big|\leq p_{\text{-}k}^{-2}r_{\text{-}k}c^{2}p_{\text{-}k}^{2}=\mathcal{O}(1).

Moreover, by Lemma
B.19
, for any
i,j\in[p_{k}]
and any
u,v\in[p_{\text{-}k}]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\left[\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\Big|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}S_{k,t}(i,j;u,v)\Big|\right]^{2}\right)\lesssim\log(T),

where the implied constant is independent of
i,j,u
or
v
. Combining the above, by Hölder inequality, we have

\displaystyle\quad\;\mathbb{E}\left(\left[\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\bigg|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\left[Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal})\right]_{ij}\bigg|\right]^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(p_{\text{-}k}^{-2}\sum_{h=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{u=1}^{p_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{v=1}^{p_{\text{-}k}}\big|\lambda_{-k,uh}\lambda_{-k,vh}\big|\right)^{2}\max_{u,v\in[p_{\text{-}k}]}\mathbb{E}\left(\left[\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\Big|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}S_{k,t}(i,j;u,v)\Big|\right]^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\log(T),

where the hidden constant does not depend on
i
and
j
, so that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{k}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left(\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\big[(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big]\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)\lesssim\log(T).

Further, by Markov’s inequality,

{\frac{1}{p_{k}}\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\big[(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big]\right\|_{F}}=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\sqrt{\log(T)}).

The same bound holds for the sums over
\{\tau+1,\ldots,e_{\ell}\}
uniformly over
\mathcal{I}
, so we conclude

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{k}}\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\max\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\left\|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\big[Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big]\right\|_{F},\right.

\displaystyle\left.\frac{1}{\sqrt{e_{\ell}-\tau}}\left\|\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e_{\ell}}\big[Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big]\right\|_{F}\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{P}\left(\sqrt{\log(T)}\right).

(B.32)

From the above, for
\tau-s_{\ell}>\varpi_{T}
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\;\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\left|\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}\cdot\mathrm{vech}\left({\mathcal{J}}_{k,s_{\ell},\tau}^{(1)}\right)\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\frac{1}{p_{k}}\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\big[(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big]\right\|_{F}}=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\sqrt{\log(T)}),

which gives

\displaystyle\quad\;\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\left|\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}\cdot{\mathcal{J}}_{s_{\ell},\tau}^{(1)}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{p_{k}}\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}{\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\big[(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal})\big]\right\|_{F}^{2}}\right)^{1/2}=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\sqrt{\log(T)}).

The same bound holds for the sums over
\{\tau+1,\ldots,e_{\ell}\}
uniformly over
\mathcal{I}
, so that we have
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{S}_{T,p}^{(1)})\to 0
.
∎

Lemma B.21
.

Let all assumptions in Lemma
B.19
hold. For some finite constant
C_{2}>0
, and with
\mathcal{I}
defined in Lemma
B.19
and
\alpha_{T,p}
defined in (
B.26
), we define

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{T,p}^{(2)}=\left\{\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\left(\Big|{\mathcal{J}}_{s_{\ell},\tau}^{(2)}\Big|_{2},\,\Big|{\mathcal{J}}_{\tau,e_{\ell}}^{(2)}\Big|_{2}\right)\geq C_{2}\alpha_{T,p}\right\},

where for any
0\leq a<b\leq T
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{J}}_{a,b}^{(2)}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{vech}\left({\mathcal{J}}_{1,a,b}^{(2)}\right)\\
\vdots\\
\mathrm{vech}\left({\mathcal{J}}_{K,a,b}^{(2)}\right)\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{vech}\left(\frac{1}{pp_{-1}}\frac{1}{b-a}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}X_{1,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{-1}\widehat{\Lambda}_{-1}^{\intercal}-{\Lambda}_{-1}{\Lambda}_{-1}^{\intercal}\right)X_{1,t}^{\intercal}\right)\\
\vdots\\
\mathrm{vech}\left(\frac{1}{pp_{-K}}\frac{1}{b-a}\sum_{t=a+1}^{b}X_{K,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{-K}\widehat{\Lambda}_{-K}^{\intercal}-{\Lambda}_{-K}{\Lambda}_{-K}^{\intercal}\right)X_{K,t}^{\intercal}\right)\end{bmatrix}.

then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big(\mathcal{S}_{T,p}^{(2)}\big)\to 0\text{\ as\ }\min(T,p_{1},\ldots,p_{K})\to\infty.

Proof of Lemma
B.21
.

For any
(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}
with
\varpi_{T}\asymp T/\log(T)
, by Lemma
B.17
(i) and Equation (
B.19
), we have

\displaystyle\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{p}\frac{1}{\tau-s_{\ell}}\Bigg\|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{p}\frac{1}{\tau-s_{\ell}}\Bigg\|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\left(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}\big(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\right)\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\qquad+\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{p}\frac{1}{\tau-s_{\ell}}\Bigg\|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\mathbb{E}\big(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{p}\frac{1}{\tau-s_{\ell}}\Bigg\|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\left(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}\big(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\right)\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\qquad+\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{p}\frac{1}{\tau-s_{\ell}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\Big\|\mathbb{E}\big(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\Big\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(T)}{\varpi_{T}}}\right)+\mathcal{O}(1)=\mathcal{O}_{P}(1).

(B.33)

Combining the above with Lemmas
B.4
(i) and Lemma
B.16
, we obtain

\displaystyle\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{\tau-s_{\ell}}\Bigg\|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}X_{k,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{\text{-}k}}\Big\|{\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\Big\|_{F}\cdot\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\frac{1}{p}\frac{1}{\tau-s_{\ell}}\Bigg\|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Bigg\|_{F}=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p,\text{-}k}).

Analogous arguments apply to the sums over
\{\tau+1,\ldots,e_{\ell}\}
uniformly over
\mathcal{I}
. Therefore, we conclude

\displaystyle\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\,

\displaystyle\left\{\Big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s_{\ell},\tau}^{(2)}\Big\|_{F},\,\Big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e_{\ell}}^{(2)}\Big\|_{F}\right\}

\displaystyle=\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}

\displaystyle\left\{\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{\tau-s_{\ell}}\Bigg\|\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}X_{k,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Bigg\|_{F}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{e_{\ell}-\tau}\Bigg\|\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e_{\ell}}X_{k,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Bigg\|_{F}\right\}=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p,\text{-}k}).

(B.34)

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\quad\;\left|\mathrm{vech}\left(\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{\tau-s_{\ell}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}X_{k,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg\|\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{\tau-s_{\ell}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}X_{k,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Bigg\|_{F},

and thus

\displaystyle\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\Big|{\mathcal{J}}_{s_{\ell},\tau}^{(2)}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{\tau-s_{\ell}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}X_{k,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Bigg\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p}),

from which we conclude that
\mathbb{P}\big(\mathcal{S}_{T,p}^{(2)}\big)\to 0
.
∎

Lemma B.22
.

Let Assumptions
1
,
2
,
’
‣
A.3.1
and
4
hold, with
\nu>4
and
\beta>1
.
Recalling that
Z_{k,t}=p_{-k}^{-1}X_{k,t}\Lambda_{-k}
, for the constant
\epsilon
satisfying
\epsilon\in(0,\nu/2-2)
, we have for any
k\in[K]
,
i,j\in[p_{k}]
:

\mathbb{E}\left(\left|\sum_{t=s+1}^{e}\left(Z_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}Z_{k,t,j\cdot}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}Z_{k,t,j\cdot})\right)\right|^{2+\epsilon}\right)\leq c_{0}(e-s)^{1+\epsilon/2},

where
c_{0}>0
is some constant that depends only on
\nu
.

Proof of Lemma
B.22
.

Using the notations in (
B.6
), we have

\displaystyle\;\quad Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal}=p_{\text{-}k}^{-2}X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}=p_{\text{-}k}^{-2}(\Lambda_{k}G_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}+E_{k,t})\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}(\Lambda_{k}G_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}+E_{k,t})^{\intercal}

\displaystyle=\Lambda_{k}G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}+p_{\text{-}k}^{-1}\Lambda_{k}G_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}+p_{\text{-}k}^{-1}E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}+p_{\text{-}k}^{-2}E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal},

so that this lemma holds true by Minkowski’s inequality if we can show

\mathcal{I}_{Z},\;\mathcal{II}_{Z},\;\mathcal{III}_{Z},\;\mathcal{IV}_{Z}\leq c_{0}(e-s)^{1+\epsilon/2},

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{Z}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\left|\sum_{t=s+1}^{e}\left(\Lambda_{k,i\cdot}^{\intercal}G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{k,j\cdot}-\mathbb{E}(\Lambda_{k,i\cdot}^{\intercal}G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{k,j\cdot})\right)\right|^{2+\epsilon}\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{II}_{Z}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\left|\sum_{t=s+1}^{e}\left(p_{\text{-}k}^{-1}\Lambda_{k,i\cdot}^{\intercal}G_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t,j\cdot}-\mathbb{E}(p_{\text{-}k}^{-1}\Lambda_{k,i\cdot}^{\intercal}G_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t,j\cdot})\right)\right|^{2+\epsilon}\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}_{Z}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\left|\sum_{t=s+1}^{e}\left(p_{\text{-}k}^{-1}E_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{k,j\cdot}-\mathbb{E}(p_{\text{-}k}^{-1}E_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{k,j\cdot})\right)\right|^{2+\epsilon}\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{IV}_{Z}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\left|\sum_{t=s+1}^{e}\left(p_{\text{-}k}^{-2}E_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t,j\cdot}-\mathbb{E}(p_{\text{-}k}^{-2}E_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t,j\cdot})\right)\right|^{2+\epsilon}\right).

We only prove the result for
\mathcal{I}_{Z}
, since all others can be shown in analogous arguments. Note that by Assumption
1
, for any
t\in[T]
,

\begin{split}&\;\quad\Lambda_{k,i\cdot}^{\intercal}G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{k,j\cdot}=\sum_{u,v\in[r_{k}]}\sum_{\ell\in[r_{\text{-}k}]}\Lambda_{k,iu}\Lambda_{k,jv}G_{k,t,u\ell}G_{k,t,v\ell}.\end{split}

(B.35)

Recall also by definition that

\displaystyle G_{k,t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{q+1}A_{j,k}F_{k,t}A_{j,\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\cdot\mathbb{I}_{\{\theta_{j-1}<t\leq\theta_{j}\}},

which, combined with (
B.35
), Lemma
B.15

(with
\beta>1
therein so as to satisfy the condition in Proposition 4 of
13
),
Assumptions
1
,
2
(ii), and
’
‣
A.3.1
(i), we can immediately conclude
\mathcal{I}_{Z}\leq c_{0}(e-s)^{1+\epsilon/2}
for some constant
c_{0}
by applying Proposition 4 in
13
.
For
\mathcal{II}_{Z}
,
\mathcal{III}_{Z}
, and
\mathcal{IV}_{Z}
, similar results follow by also using Lemma
B.15
and applying Proposition 4 in
13
, except that we require Assumption
4
in addition. This concludes the proof of this lemma.
∎

Lemma B.23
.

Let all assumptions in Lemma
B.22
hold, except that Assumption
’
‣
A.3.1
is replaced by Assumption
3
.
Then for the same constant
\epsilon
in Lemma
B.22
, we have for any
k\in[K]
and
\kappa_{T}\to\infty
arbitrarily slowly,

\mathbb{P}\left\{\max_{j\in[q+1]}\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\frac{\sqrt{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}}}{p_{k}\ell}\left\|\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}\left(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal})\right)\right\|_{F}\geq\kappa_{T}\right\}=o(1).

(B.36)

Proof of Lemma
B.23
.

By Minkowski’s inequality, we have for any
k\in[K]
,
w\in[q+1]
, and
i,j\in[p_{k}]
,

\displaystyle\;\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\max_{\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{w}-\theta_{w-1}}\frac{\sqrt{\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}}}{\ell}\left|\sum_{t=\theta_{w}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{w}}\left(Z_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}Z_{k,t,j\cdot}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}Z_{k,t,j\cdot})\right)\right|\right)^{2+\epsilon}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\max_{\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{w}-\theta_{w-1}}\frac{\sqrt{\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}}}{\ell}\left|\sum_{t=\theta_{w}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{w}-\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}+1}\left(Z_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}Z_{k,t,j\cdot}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}Z_{k,t,j\cdot})\right)\right|\right)^{2+\epsilon}\right]

\displaystyle\;\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\max_{\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{w}-\theta_{w-1}}\frac{\sqrt{\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}}}{\ell}\left|\sum_{t=\theta_{w}-\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}+2}^{\theta_{w}}\left(Z_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}Z_{k,t,j\cdot}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}Z_{k,t,j\cdot})\right)\right|\right)^{2+\epsilon}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\right)^{1+\epsilon/2}\sum_{\ell=\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}}^{\theta_{w}-\theta_{w-1}}\frac{1}{\ell^{2+\epsilon}}\cdot\ell^{\epsilon/2}+\left\{\left(\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\right)^{-1}\left(\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}-1\right)\right\}^{1+\epsilon/2}=\mathcal{O}(1),

where the last line used Lemma
B.22
in applying Theorem B.3 in
39
; the first term in the last line can be upper bounded by an integral inequality; and the second term is trivially bounded, with
\mathcal{O}(1)
that does not depend on
i
,
j
,
w
or
k
. This shows that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\max_{\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{w}-\theta_{w-1}}\frac{\sqrt{\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}}}{\ell}\left|\sum_{t=\theta_{w}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{w}}\left(Z_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}Z_{k,t,j\cdot}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}Z_{k,t,j\cdot})\right)\right|\right)^{2+\epsilon}\right]=\mathcal{O}(1).

(B.37)

Next, by Jensen’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\;\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\max_{\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{w}-\theta_{w-1}}\frac{\sqrt{\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}}}{p_{k}\ell}\left\|\sum_{t=\theta_{w}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{w}}\left(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\intercal})\right)\right\|_{F}\right)^{2+\epsilon}\right]

\displaystyle=\!\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\max_{\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{w}-\theta_{w-1}}\frac{1}{p_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i,j\in[p_{k}]}\left|\frac{\sqrt{\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}}}{\ell}\sum_{t=\theta_{w}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{w}}\left(Z_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}Z_{k,t,j\cdot}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}Z_{k,t,j\cdot})\right)\right|^{2}\right)^{1+\epsilon/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\!\frac{1}{p_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i,j\in[p_{k}]}\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{w}-\theta_{w-1}}\left|\frac{\sqrt{\omega_{w}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}}}{\ell}\sum_{t=\theta_{w}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{w}}\left(Z_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}Z_{k,t,j\cdot}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}Z_{k,t,j\cdot})\right)\right|^{2+\epsilon}\right]=\mathcal{O}(1),

where the last equality used (
B.37
). Finally, it holds by the Markov’s inequality that

\displaystyle\;\quad\mathbb{P}\left\{\max_{j\in[q+1]}\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\frac{\sqrt{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}}}{p_{k}\ell}\left\|\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}\left(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\top})\right)\right\|_{F}\geq\kappa_{T}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{q}{(\kappa_{T})^{2+\epsilon}}\max_{j\in[q+1]}\mathbb{E}\left\{\left(\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\frac{\sqrt{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}}}{p_{k}\ell}\left\|\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}\left(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}(Z_{k,t}Z_{k,t}^{\top})\right)\right\|_{F}\right)^{2+\epsilon}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{q}{(\kappa_{T})^{2+\epsilon}}=o(1),

where the last equality holds as
q
is fixed due to linearly spaced change points and
\kappa_{T}
goes to infinity (arbitrarily slowly). This completes the proof of this lemma.
∎

Lemma B.24
.

Let Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
and
4
hold, with
\nu>8
and
\beta\geq 1
. Then for
\kappa_{T}\to\infty
arbitrarily slowly, with
{\mathcal{J}}_{a,b}^{(1)}
defined in Lemma
B.20
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\widetilde{\mathcal{S}}_{T,p}^{(1)}\right)\to 0\text{ \ as \ }\min(T,p_{1},\ldots,p_{K})\to\infty,

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{S}}_{T,p}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\left\{\max_{1\leq j\leq q+1}\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\sqrt{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}}\Big|{\mathcal{J}}_{\theta_{j}-\ell,\theta_{j}}^{(1)}\Big|_{2}\geq\kappa_{T}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\bigcap\left\{\max_{0\leq j\leq q}\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j+1}-\theta_{j}}\sqrt{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}}\Big|{\mathcal{J}}_{\theta_{j},\theta_{j}+\ell}^{(1)}\Big|_{2}\geq\kappa_{T}\right\}.

Also, with
{\mathcal{J}}_{a,b}^{(2)}
defined as in Lemma
B.21
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\widetilde{\mathcal{S}}_{T,p}^{(2)}\right)\to 0\text{ \ as \ }\min(T,p_{1},\ldots,p_{K})\to\infty,

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{S}}_{T,p}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=\left\{\max_{1\leq j\leq q+1}\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\Big|{\mathcal{J}}_{\theta_{j}-\ell,\theta_{j}}^{(2)}\Big|_{2}\geq\widetilde{C}_{2}\alpha_{T,p}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\bigcap\left\{\max_{0\leq j\leq q}\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j+1}-\theta_{j}}\Big|{\mathcal{J}}_{\theta_{j},\theta_{j}+\ell}^{(2)}\Big|_{2}\geq\widetilde{C}_{2}\alpha_{T,p}\right\},

with some finite constant
\widetilde{C}_{2}>0
.

Proof of Lemma
B.24
.

Following the same line of reasoning as in the proof of Lemma
B.22
, for the constant
\epsilon\in(0,\nu/2-2)
, we have for any
k\in[K]
and
i,j\in[p_{k}]
,

\mathbb{E}\left(\left|\sum_{t=s+1}^{e}\left(p_{\text{-}k}^{-1}E_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}E_{k,t,j\cdot}-\mathbb{E}(p_{\text{-}k}^{-1}E_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}E_{k,t,j\cdot})\right)\right|^{2+\epsilon}\right)\leq c_{0}(e-s)^{1+\epsilon/2},

with some constant
c_{0}
depending on
\nu
.
Noting that

\displaystyle p_{\text{-}k}^{-1}X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}=\Lambda_{k}G_{k,t}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}+p_{\text{-}k}^{-1}\Lambda_{k}G_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}E_{k,t}^{\intercal}+p_{\text{-}k}^{-1}E_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}G_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Lambda_{k}^{\intercal}+p_{\text{-}k}^{-1}E_{k,t}E_{k,t}^{\intercal},

together with
\mathcal{I}_{Z},\;\mathcal{II}_{Z},\;\mathcal{III}_{Z}\leq c_{0}(e-s)^{1+\epsilon/2}
derived in the proof of Lemma
B.22
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\left|\frac{1}{p_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t=s+1}^{e}\left(X_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}X_{k,t,j\cdot}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t,i\cdot}^{\intercal}X_{k,t,j\cdot})\right)\right|^{2+\epsilon}\right)\leq c_{0}(e-s)^{1+\epsilon/2}.

Then, by the same argument as in the proof of Lemma
B.23
, for
\kappa_{T}\to\infty
arbitrarily slowly, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\max_{1\leq j\leq q+1}\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\frac{\sqrt{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}}}{p\,\ell}\left\|\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}\left(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal})\right)\right\|_{F}\geq\kappa_{T}\right\}=o(1),

and

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq q+1}\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\frac{\omega_{j}^{-1}}{p\,\ell}\left\|\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}\left(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal})\right)\right\|_{F}=\mathcal{O}_{P}(1).

(B.38)

Analogous arguments apply to the sums over
\{\theta_{j}+1,\ldots,\theta_{j}+\ell\}
and show

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left\{\max_{0\leq j\leq q}\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j+1}-\theta_{j}}\frac{\sqrt{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}}}{p\,\ell}\left\|\sum_{t=\theta_{j}+1}^{\theta_{j}+\ell}\left(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal})\right)\right\|_{F}\geq\kappa_{T}\right\}=o(1).

Then, noting that
{\mathcal{J}}_{a,b}^{(1)}
is obtained by stacking
\mathrm{vech}({\mathcal{J}}_{k,a,b}^{(1)})
, for
k\in[K]
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\widetilde{\mathcal{S}}_{T,p}^{(1)}\right)\to 0\text{ \ as \ }\min(T,p_{1},\ldots,p_{K})\to\infty.

We now prove the second claim. For any
j\in[q+1]
, by Lemma
B.17
(i) and (
B.38
),

\displaystyle\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\frac{1}{p\,\ell}\Bigg\|\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\frac{1}{p\,\ell}\Bigg\|\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}\left(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}\big(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\right)\Bigg\|_{F}+

\displaystyle\qquad\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\frac{1}{p\,\ell}\Bigg\|\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}\mathbb{E}\big(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\frac{1}{p\,\ell}\Bigg\|\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}\left(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}\big(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\right)\Bigg\|_{F}+

\displaystyle\qquad\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\frac{1}{p\,\ell}\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}\Big\|\mathbb{E}\big(X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\big)\Big\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{P}\left(\omega_{j}\right)+\mathcal{O}(1)=\mathcal{O}_{P}(1),

where the last equality follows from the fact that

\displaystyle\omega_{j}\leq 2\max_{j^{\prime}\in\{j,j-1\},\ j\in[q+1]}\left|\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{vech}\left(\Gamma_{G,(j^{\prime})}^{(1)}\right)\\
\vdots\\
\mathrm{vech}\left(\Gamma_{G,(j^{\prime})}^{(K)}\right)\end{bmatrix}\right|_{2}=\mathcal{O}(1).

(B.39)

Combining the above with Lemmas
B.4
(i) and Lemma
B.16
, we obtain

\displaystyle\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}\,\ell}\Bigg\|\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}X_{k,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p_{\text{-}k}}\Big\|{\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\Big\|_{F}\cdot\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\frac{1}{p\,\ell}\Bigg\|\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}X_{k,t}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Bigg\|_{F}=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p,\text{-}k}),

where the LHS does not depend on
j\in[q+1]
, hence

\max_{1\leq j\leq q+1}\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}\,\ell}\Bigg\|\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}X_{k,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Bigg\|_{F}=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p,\text{-}k}).

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\quad\;\left|\mathrm{vech}\left(\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}\,\ell}\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}X_{k,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg\|\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}\,\ell}\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}X_{k,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Bigg\|_{F},

and thus

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq q+1}\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\Big|{\mathcal{J}}_{\theta_{j}-\ell,\theta_{j}}^{(2)}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{1\leq j\leq q+1}\max_{\omega_{j}^{-2}\kappa_{T}^{2}\leq\ell\leq\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1}}\!\!\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left\|\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}\,\ell}\sum_{t=\theta_{j}-\ell+1}^{\theta_{j}}X_{k,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\!\!=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p}).

Analogous arguments apply to the sums over
\{\theta_{j}+1,\ldots,\theta_{j}+\ell\}
from which we conclude that
\mathbb{P}\big(\widetilde{\mathcal{S}}_{T,p}^{(2)}\big)\to 0
.
∎

Lemma B.25
.

Under Model (
2.2
), consider
(s,e)
which satisfies
0\leq s<e\leq T
and
\{\theta_{j}\}\subset\{s{+}1,\ldots,e{-}1\}\cap\Theta\subset\{\theta_{j},\theta_{j+1}\}
for some
j\in[q]
. Define

\displaystyle\bar{V}_{t}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\bar{V}^{(1)}_{t}\\
\vdots\\
\bar{V}^{(K)}_{t}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{vech}\left(\mathbb{E}(G_{1,t}G_{1,t}^{\intercal})\right)\\
\vdots\\
\mathrm{vech}\left(\mathbb{E}(G_{K,t}G_{K,t}^{\intercal})\right)\end{bmatrix},\quad\Omega_{j}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{vech}\big(\Omega_{j}^{(1)}\big)\\
\vdots\\
\mathrm{vech}\big(\Omega_{j}^{(K)}\big)\end{bmatrix}\text{ \ for each \ }j\in[q],

and

\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{V}}_{s,\tau,e}=\begin{bmatrix}\bar{\mathcal{V}}^{(1)}_{s,\tau,e}\\
\vdots\\
\bar{\mathcal{V}}^{(K)}_{s,\tau,e}\end{bmatrix}=\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s)(e-\tau)}{e-s}}\left(\frac{1}{e-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e}\bar{V}_{t}-\frac{1}{\tau-s}\sum_{t=s+1}^{\tau}\bar{V}_{t}\right),

with which we denote

\mathcal{T}^{*}_{s,\tau,e}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathcal{T}^{*(1)}_{s,\tau,e}\\
\vdots\\
\mathcal{T}^{*(K)}_{s,\tau,e}\end{bmatrix}=\left|\bar{\mathcal{V}}_{s,\tau,e}\right|_{2}.

Then we have
\argmax_{s<\tau<e}\mathcal{T}^{*}_{s,\tau,e}\in\{\theta_{j},\theta_{j+1}\}\cap\{s+1,\ldots,e-1\}
, and

\displaystyle\max_{s<\tau<e}\mathcal{T}^{*}_{s,\tau,e}=\max\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{(\theta_{j}-s)(e-\theta_{j})}{e-s}}\,\Big|\Omega_{j}+\frac{e-\theta_{j+1}}{e-\theta_{j}}\,\Omega_{j+1}\,\mathbb{I}_{\{\theta_{j+1}<e)\}}\Big|_{2},

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{(\theta_{j-1}-s)(e-\theta_{j-1})}{e-s}}\,\Big|\Omega_{j+1}+\frac{\theta_{j}-s}{\theta_{j+1}-s}\,\Omega_{j}\Big|_{2}\,\mathbb{I}_{\{\theta_{j+1}<e)\}}\Bigg\}.

Proof of Lemma
B.25
.

Under (
2.2
), the sequence
\{\bar{V}_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T}
is piecewise constant with all the change points belonging to
\Theta
.
Using the dual characterization of the Euclidean norm, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{*}_{s,\tau,e}=|\bar{\mathcal{V}}_{s,\tau,e}|_{2}=\sup_{|\mathbf{g}|_{2}=1}\mathbf{g}^{\intercal}\bar{\mathcal{V}}_{s,\tau,e}.

Let
\tau^{*}\in\argmax_{s<\tau<e}\mathcal{T}^{*}_{s,\tau,e}
and choose
\mathbf{g}^{*}
with
|\mathbf{g}^{*}|_{2}=1
such that

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{*}_{s,\tau^{*},e}=(\mathbf{g}^{*})^{\intercal}\bar{\mathcal{V}}_{s,\tau^{*},e}.

Then
\tau^{*}
also maximizes
(\mathbf{g}^{*})^{\intercal}\bar{\mathcal{V}}_{s,\tau,e}
over
s<\tau<e
; otherwise there exists
\tau^{**}
with
(\mathbf{g}^{*})^{\intercal}\bar{\mathcal{V}}_{s,\tau^{**},e}>(\mathbf{g}^{*})^{\intercal}\bar{\mathcal{V}}_{s,\tau^{*},e}=\mathcal{T}^{*}_{s,\tau^{*},e}
, which implies
\mathcal{T}^{*}_{s,\tau^{**},e}\geq(\mathbf{g}^{*})^{\intercal}\bar{\mathcal{V}}_{s,\tau^{**},e}>\mathcal{T}^{*}_{s,\tau^{*},e}
, which is a contradiction.
Note that for fixed
\mathbf{g}^{*}
, the scalar sequence
\{(\mathbf{g}^{*})^{\intercal}\bar{V}_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T}
is piecewise constant with all its change points within
\Theta
.
Therefore, by Lemma 8 of
56
, we have

\displaystyle\tau^{*}\in\{\theta_{j},\theta_{j+1}\}\cap\{s+1,\ldots,e-1\}.

The second statement follows from evaluating
\mathcal{T}^{*}_{s,\tau,e}
at
\tau=\theta_{j}
and
\tau=\theta_{j+1}
(in case
\theta_{j+1}<e)
.
∎

Lemma B.26
.

Let all assumptions in Lemma
B.19
hold.
Recalling that
\Theta
denotes the set of true change points,
with the definition of
V_{t}
from (
B.30
),
we have

\displaystyle\quad\;\big|\widehat{\mathcal{V}}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}-{\mathcal{V}}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s_{\ell})(e_{\ell}-\tau)}{e_{\ell}-s_{\ell}}}\left|\frac{1}{e_{\ell}-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e_{\ell}}\left(\widehat{V}_{t}-V_{t}\right)-\frac{1}{\tau-s_{\ell}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\left(\widehat{V}_{t}-V_{t}\right)\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s_{\ell})(e_{\ell}-\tau)}{e_{\ell}-s_{\ell}}}+\mathcal{T}^{*}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}\right)\cdot\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p})+\mathcal{O}_{P}\big(\sqrt{\log(T)}\big),

where
\alpha_{T,p}
is defined in (
B.26
) and
\mathcal{T}^{*}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}
in Lemma
B.25
; throughout, the
\mathcal{O}_{P}
-bounds hold uniformly over
(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}
, with
\mathcal{I}
as defined in Lemma
B.19
.
Further, suppose that Assumption
6
holds. Then the result simplifies to

\displaystyle\big|\widehat{\mathcal{V}}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}-{\mathcal{V}}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}\big|_{2}=\mathcal{O}_{P}\big(\sqrt{\log(T)}\big).

Proof of Lemma
B.26
.

Recall from Lemma
B.18
that

\displaystyle\quad\;\big|\widehat{\mathcal{V}}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}^{(k)}-{\mathcal{V}}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}^{(k)}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s_{\ell})(e_{\ell}-\tau)}{e_{\ell}-s_{\ell}}}\left(\Big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e_{\ell}}^{(1)}\Big\|_{F}+\Big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s_{\ell},\tau}^{(1)}\Big\|_{F}+\Big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e_{\ell}}^{(2)}\Big\|_{F}+\Big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s_{\ell},\tau}^{(2)}\Big\|_{F}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\,+\,2\sqrt{2}\|\widehat{H}_{k}\|\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{k}}}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\right)\right\|_{F}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s)(e-\tau)}{e-s}}\cdot\left|\mathrm{vech}\left(\Gamma_{G,\tau,e}^{(k)}-\Gamma_{G,s,\tau}^{(k)}\right)\right|_{2},

\displaystyle=:U_{k,1}+U_{k,2}.

To control for
U_{k,1}
, first note that by (
B.32
),

\displaystyle\quad\;\Big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e_{\ell}}^{(1)}\Big\|_{F}+\Big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s_{\ell},\tau}^{(1)}\Big\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{e_{\ell}-\tau}}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{e_{\ell}-\tau}}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e_{\ell}}\left[X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}\left(X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)\right]\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\left[X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}-\mathbb{E}\left(X_{k,t}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\right)\right]\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{e_{\ell}-\tau}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\tau-s_{\ell}}}\right)\cdot\mathcal{O}_{P}\big(\sqrt{\log(T)}\big),

while by (
B.34
),

\displaystyle\Big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,\tau,e_{\ell}}^{(2)}\Big\|_{F}+\Big\|{\mathcal{J}}_{k,s_{\ell},\tau}^{(2)}\Big\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\Bigg\|\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{e_{\ell}-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e_{\ell}}X_{k,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\,\Bigg\|\frac{1}{pp_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{\tau-s_{\ell}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}X_{k,t}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}-\Lambda_{\text{-}k}\Lambda_{\text{-}k}^{\intercal}\right)X_{k,t}^{\intercal}\Bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p,\text{-}k}),

all uniformly over
(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}
.
This gives

\displaystyle U_{k,1}=\mathcal{O}_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s_{\ell})(e_{\ell}-\tau)}{e_{\ell}-s_{\ell}}}\cdot\alpha_{T,p}+\sqrt{\log(T)}\right).

Also, recalling
\mathcal{T}^{*(k)}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}
defined in Lemma
B.25
, we can write

\displaystyle U_{k,2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{2}\|\widehat{H}_{k}\|\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{k}}}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}-\Lambda_{k}\widehat{H}_{k}\right)\right\|_{F}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s_{\ell})(e_{\ell}-\tau)}{e_{\ell}-s_{\ell}}}\left|\mathrm{vech}\left(\Gamma_{G,\tau,e}^{(k)}-\Gamma_{G,s,\tau}^{(k)}\right)\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}^{*(k)}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}\cdot\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p,k})

uniformly for
(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}
, by Lemma
B.16
(ii).
Combining the bounds on
U_{k,1}
and
U_{k,2}
, and stacking
\widehat{\mathcal{V}}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}^{(k)}
, for
k\in[K]
,

\displaystyle\quad\;\big|\widehat{\mathcal{V}}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}-{\mathcal{V}}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s_{\ell})(e_{\ell}-\tau)}{e_{\ell}-s_{\ell}}}\cdot\left\{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left|\frac{1}{e_{\ell}-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e_{\ell}}\left(\widehat{V}^{(k)}_{t}-V^{(k)}_{t}\right)-\frac{1}{\tau-s_{\ell}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}\left(\widehat{V}^{(k)}_{t}-V^{(k)}_{t}\right)\right|_{2}^{2}\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\left(\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s_{\ell})(e_{\ell}-\tau)}{e_{\ell}-s_{\ell}}}+\mathcal{T}^{*}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}\right)\cdot\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p})+\mathcal{O}_{P}\big(\sqrt{\log(T)}\big).

Further, under Assumption
6
, it holds that
\sqrt{T}\alpha_{T,p}=\mathcal{O}(\log(T))
, from which the simplified statement follows.
∎

Lemma B.27
.

Let the assumptions of Lemma
B.26
hold. Uniformly over
(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}
, with
\mathcal{I}
defined in Lemma
B.19
and
V_{t}
defined in (
B.30
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{V}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}|_{2}=\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s_{\ell})(e_{\ell}-\tau)}{e_{\ell}-s_{\ell}}}\left|\frac{1}{e_{\ell}-\tau}\sum_{t=\tau+1}^{e_{\ell}}V_{t}-\frac{1}{\tau-s_{\ell}}\sum_{t=s_{\ell}+1}^{\tau}V_{t}\right|_{2}=\mathcal{T}^{*}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}\cdot(1+\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p})),

where the
\mathcal{O}_{P}
-bound depends on a quantity that does not vary with
(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})
.

Proof of Lemma
B.27
.

Recalling
\mathcal{V}^{(k)}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}
defined in (
B.31
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{V}^{(k)}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s_{\ell})(e_{\ell}-\tau)}{e_{\ell}-s_{\ell}}}\bigg|\mathrm{vech}\left\{\widehat{H}_{k}^{\intercal}\Bigl(\Gamma^{(k)}_{G,\tau,e_{\ell}}-\Gamma^{(k)}_{G,s_{\ell},\tau}\Bigr)\widehat{H}_{k}\right\}\bigg|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s_{\ell})(e_{\ell}-\tau)}{e_{\ell}-s_{\ell}}}\Big\|\widehat{H}_{k}^{\intercal}\bigl(\Gamma^{(k)}_{G,\tau,e_{\ell}}-\Gamma^{(k)}_{G,s_{\ell},\tau}\bigr)\widehat{H}_{k}\Big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s_{\ell})(e_{\ell}-\tau)}{e_{\ell}-s_{\ell}}}\big\|\widehat{H}_{k}\big\|^{2}\Big\|\Gamma^{(k)}_{G,\tau,e_{\ell}}-\Gamma^{(k)}_{G,s_{\ell},\tau}\Big\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{(\tau-s_{\ell})(e_{\ell}-\tau)}{e_{\ell}-s_{\ell}}}\sqrt{2}\Big|\mathrm{vech}\big(\Gamma^{(k)}_{G,\tau,e_{\ell}}-\Gamma^{(k)}_{G,s_{\ell},\tau}\big)\Big|_{2}\cdot\big\|\widehat{H}_{k}\big\|^{2}

(B.40)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}^{*(k)}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}\cdot(1+\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p,k})),

where
\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p,k})
is due to the bounding of
\|\widehat{H}_{k}\|
by Lemma
B.16
(ii), and thus does not depend on
(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})
.
This carries over with
\mathcal{V}^{(k)}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}
, for
k\in[K]
, stacked over, so that uniformly over
(s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell})\in\mathcal{I}
,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{V}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}|_{2}=\mathcal{T}^{*}_{s_{\ell},\tau,e_{\ell}}\cdot(1+\mathcal{O}_{P}(\alpha_{T,p})).

∎

B.3.2
Proof of Theorem
2

By assumption,
W_{\tau}=W
in (
3.3
) is a positive definite matrix with bounded eigenvalues.
WLOG, we regard
W=I
so that the detector statistic becomes
\mathcal{T}_{s,\tau,e}=\big|\widehat{\mathcal{V}}_{s,\tau,e}\big|_{2}
.
Throughout, we regard that
\upsilon_{T}=\kappa_{T}^{2}
for the sequence
\kappa_{T}\to\infty
from Lemma
B.23
, and that
\kappa_{T}
satisfies

\displaystyle\kappa_{T}^{2}=o\left\{\min\left(\min_{j\in[q]}\omega_{j}^{2}\Delta_{j}/\log(T),\,\log(T)\right)\right\},

(B.41)

which is permitted as it is only required to diverge arbitrarily slowly.

For all
j\in[q]
, recall that
\Delta_{j}=\min(\theta_{j}-\theta_{j-1},\theta_{j+1}-\theta_{j})
. Supposing that
\Delta_{j}>12
, we define the following sets

\displaystyle\underline{\mathcal{I}}_{j}=\left\{\theta_{j}-\Big\lfloor\frac{\Delta_{j}}{3}\Big\rfloor,\theta_{j}-\Big\lfloor\frac{\Delta_{j}}{3}\Big\rfloor+1,\dots,\theta_{j}-\Big\lceil\frac{\Delta_{j}}{12}\Big\rceil\right\},

\displaystyle\overline{\mathcal{I}}_{j}=\left\{\theta_{j}+\Big\lceil\frac{\Delta_{j}}{12}\Big\rceil,\theta_{j}+\Big\lceil\frac{\Delta_{j}}{12}\Big\rceil+1,\dots,\theta_{j}+\Big\lfloor\frac{\Delta_{j}}{3}\Big\rfloor\right\}.

Then under Assumption
3
(iv), we always have the following event hold:

\mathcal{M}_{T,M}=\Big\{\text{For each $j\in[q]$, there is some $(a,b]\in\mathbb{M}$ such that $(a,b)\in\underline{\mathcal{I}}_{j}\times\overline{\mathcal{I}}_{j}$}\Big\},

(B.42)

due to the reasoning sketched as follows. For simplicity, we treat floor and ceiling functions as identity transforms. By construction of
\mathbb{M}
in (
3.4
), there exists
\ell\in[\mu_{T}]
such that
2m_{\ell}\in[\Delta_{j}/6,2\Delta_{j}/3]
with
m_{\ell}=T/2^{\ell}
, or equivalently,
\Delta_{j}\in[3m_{\ell},12m_{\ell}]
, noticing that
m_{\ell}=2m_{\ell+1}
.
At such level
\ell
, we identify
(a,b]\in\mathbb{M}
with
a\in\underline{\mathcal{I}}_{j}
and
b\in\overline{\mathcal{I}}_{j}
while
b=a+2m_{\ell}
, so that

\displaystyle\theta_{j}-\frac{\Delta_{j}}{3}\leq a

\displaystyle\leq\theta_{j}-\frac{\Delta_{j}}{12}\text{\ and\ }\theta_{j}+\frac{\Delta_{j}}{12}\leq b\leq\theta_{j}+\frac{\Delta_{j}}{3}.

Re-arranging the above, we identify
a
satisfying

a\in\left[\theta_{j}-\frac{\Delta_{j}}{3},\theta_{j}-\frac{\Delta_{j}}{12}\right]\cap\left[\theta_{j}+\frac{\Delta_{j}}{12}-2m_{\ell},\theta_{j}+\frac{\Delta_{j}}{3}-2m_{\ell}\right],

where the intersection is non-empty if

\theta_{j}-\frac{\Delta_{j}}{3}\leq\theta_{j}+\frac{\Delta_{j}}{3}-2m_{\ell}\text{\ and\ }\theta_{j}+\frac{\Delta_{j}}{12}-2m_{\ell}\leq\theta_{j}-\frac{\Delta_{j}}{12},

which is fulfilled by
2m_{\ell}\in[\Delta_{j}/6,2\Delta_{j}/3]
. It remains to match this
a
to the left endpoint of intervals in
\mathbb{M}
. To this end, roughly speaking (up to integer rounding), we can set
i=a/m_{\ell}+1
so that
(\lfloor(i-1)m_{\ell}\rfloor,\lceil(i+1)m_{\ell}\rceil]=(a,a+2m_{\ell}]
which is in
\mathbb{M}
since
i\in[T/m_{\ell}-1]
due to
a+2m_{\ell}\in[T]
. This completes the sketch of proof that (
B.42
) holds.

Throughout the proof, we regard all
\mathcal{O}_{P}
-bounds as holding deterministically on the event
\big({\mathcal{H}}_{T,p}\cup\mathcal{S}^{(1)}_{T,p}\cup\mathcal{S}^{(2)}_{T,p}\cup\widetilde{\mathcal{S}}^{(1)}_{T,p}\cup\widetilde{\mathcal{S}}^{(2)}_{T,p}\big)^{c}
defined in Lemmas
B.16
(ii),
B.20
,
B.21
and
B.24
.
Let us consider some
(s,e)\subset[0,T]
which satisfy the following conditions:

(S1)

The set

\mathbb{C}_{s,e}\neq\emptyset
, where

\displaystyle\mathbb{C}_{s,e}=\left\{1\leq j\leq q:\,\exists\,(a,b]\in\mathbb{M}\text{ \ such that \ }s\leq a<b\leq e\text{ and }(a,b)\in\underline{I}_{j}\times\overline{I}_{j}\right\}.

(S2)

There exists some
j\in\{0,\ldots,q\}
and
j^{\prime}\in\{1,\ldots,q+1\}
, such that

\displaystyle\max\left\{\omega_{j}^{2}|s-\theta_{j}|,\,\omega_{j^{\prime}}^{2}|e-\theta_{j^{\prime}}|\right\}=\mathcal{O}_{P}(\kappa_{T}^{2}).

Then we show that for
(s,e)
meeting
(S1)
and
(S2)
:

(R1)

There exists at least one
\ell\in\mathbb{L}_{s,e}
for which
